Everyone is the creator of one's own fate Posts: 212 Thank you received: 100 What is necessary to trade this Kink? For a start, it is necessary to know that we have to wait in this zone. that is "approach" and "reaction". So Present. Approach. How is price returning to the zone? Where's the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? This is your tg1 in HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT this TF. Flags in the LTFs? What does PA tell you? Has price tested the last flag on approach? (good sign) Has price compressed into the zone in this TF or LTFs? (good sign) Is there big news on the way? Has there just been big news? 1- Where's the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? If at us monthly kink, means we have to trade on the day chart. (Why not? =) ) It turns out, the first that we have to wait for is a Flag on the day schedule. But from where we have to form it? How do we have to form it? How do we learn where there is a purpose of this flag? All this to us will be answered by PA! But at the same time, we have a break on the Daily chart. That says this zone we can trade too. We have to wait for a flag on H1 Where's the nearest flag in the TF you want to trade? This is your tg1 in this TF. Flags in the LTFs? It turns out that the price has to estimate this flag, to be exact the price has to fulfill this flag. So, we have to form a flag on H1 and trade above this flag for the purpose of inside new flag. look: HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT we will consider our daily Kink in more detail: Before beginning trade - we have to know where there are our targets. Offline i'd like to give a bit of advice about how you can integrate PAZ concept in Trader your trading: FORUM MORE LOGOUT HOME EDUCATION Posts: 72 Regarding the PAZ concept - it is a new dimension added to our analysis to Thank you received: make reading easier - and it was very usefull when you are drawing your trade "arena" using FL's - so basically a PAZ is limited by FL's of the same 233 scale and the way you can trade them is when price doesn't reach it's target -> the other end of PAZ in the same scale. That's why you have to "trade only the edges" When price engulfs into a new PAZ (engulfs the FL that is it's border") and doesn't reach the end of it you can enter a trade on retrace and put your SL above the FL that originated the move (the Think Tank in your charts). A simpler representation of PAZ's you will find in the QM lite course when IF taught us about the space between FL ....In the course aren't called PAZ's because it would be another term that would complicate our understanding but that's the concept : The space between FL's. This implies that we have a very good eye when drawing the PAZ's and their FL's (for example in your post your CP PAZ has actually a few important zones in it and it's not really a PAZ, because in a "perfect" CP PAZ price should visit each MPL on the way(pick up the clean orders on the way UP), leaving little or no clean orders behind so that price can travel FREE when it returns to the other end - that's why it's called a PAZ because if price engulfs into it- it travels all the way to the end of it, with or without the necessary retrace that would allow us to join the ride ). The simplest way to start drawing your PAZ's is to find your CP on the chart and then follow the FL trail. Paz's don't align to the PIP because every time an old FL that was the PAZ border gets Engulfed and a new FL is created - this new FL is a bit higher or lower than the original FL it Engulfed. In the recent years some of the IT's have changed a bit the way they mess up with the retailers to fill their position and achieve their plans: they use LS and their Kinks for their entries and for their targets and it's pretty hard to find space between FL's (PAZ) ( most of the engulfs reach their target in the same move leaving us with no PA confirmed opportunity to trade) . In the Very low time frames there are still PA confirmed entries but they require a lot of patience and wisdom. BUT There are times when LS Kinks align with FL's and you can find clean orders inside them close to the MPL(that's your edge of the PAZ) (that's your sweet spot where you want to trade) That's why you'll see that RTM has shifted focus more material HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT about BSZ and the Traps set by IT and LSK with clean orders inside it in the recent years as it's a more efficient way to extract "safer" pips - As traders our main job is to go where the Opportunities will be...and to adapt our styles to our personality and to the market that we have inherited. Stay thirsty, E. Offline i'd like to give a bit of advice about how you can integrate PAZ concept in Trader your trading: FORUM MORE LOGOUT HOME EDUCATION Posts: 72 Regarding the PAZ concept - it is a new dimension added to our analysis to Thank you received: make reading easier - and it was very usefull when you are drawing your trade "arena" using FL's - so basically a PAZ is limited by FL's of the same 233 scale and the way you can trade them is when price doesn't reach it's target -> the other end of PAZ in the same scale. That's why you have to "trade only the edges" When price engulfs into a new PAZ (engulfs the FL that is it's border") and doesn't reach the end of it you can enter a trade on retrace and put your SL above the FL that originated the move (the Think Tank in your charts). A simpler representation of PAZ's you will find in the QM lite course when IF taught us about the space between FL ....In the course aren't called PAZ's because it would be another term that would complicate our understanding but that's the concept : The space between FL's. This implies that we have a very good eye when drawing the PAZ's and their FL's (for example in your post your CP PAZ has actually a few important zones in it and it's not really a PAZ, because in a "perfect" CP PAZ price should visit each MPL on the way(pick up the clean orders on the way UP), leaving little or no clean orders behind so that price can travel FREE when it returns to the other end - that's why it's called a PAZ because if price engulfs into it- it travels all the way to the end of it, with or without the necessary retrace that would allow us to join the ride ). The simplest way to start drawing your PAZ's is to find your CP on the chart and then follow the FL trail. Paz's don't align to the PIP because every time an old FL that was the PAZ border gets Engulfed and a new FL is created - this new FL is a bit higher or lower than the original FL it Engulfed. In the recent years some of the IT's have changed a bit the way they mess up with the retailers to fill their position and achieve their plans: they use LS and their Kinks for their entries and for their targets and it's pretty hard to find space between FL's (PAZ) ( most of the engulfs reach their target in the same move leaving us with no PA confirmed opportunity to trade) . In the Very low time frames there are still PA confirmed entries but they require a lot of patience and wisdom. BUT There are times when LS Kinks align with FL's and you can find clean orders inside them close to the MPL(that's your edge of the PAZ) (that's your sweet spot where you want to trade) That's why you'll see that RTM has shifted focus more material HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT about BSZ and the Traps set by IT and LSK with clean orders inside it in the recent years as it's a more efficient way to extract "safer" pips - As traders our main job is to go where the Opportunities will be...and to adapt our styles to our personality and to the market that we have inherited. Stay thirsty, E. Offline i'd like to give a bit of advice about how you can integrate PAZ concept in Trader your trading: FORUM MORE LOGOUT HOME EDUCATION Posts: 72 Regarding the PAZ concept - it is a new dimension added to our analysis to Thank you received: make reading easier - and it was very usefull when you are drawing your trade "arena" using FL's - so basically a PAZ is limited by FL's of the same 233 scale and the way you can trade them is when price doesn't reach it's target -> the other end of PAZ in the same scale. That's why you have to "trade only the edges" When price engulfs into a new PAZ (engulfs the FL that is it's border") and doesn't reach the end of it you can enter a trade on retrace and put your SL above the FL that originated the move (the Think Tank in your charts). A simpler representation of PAZ's you will find in the QM lite course when IF taught us about the space between FL ....In the course aren't called PAZ's because it would be another term that would complicate our understanding but that's the concept : The space between FL's. This implies that we have a very good eye when drawing the PAZ's and their FL's (for example in your post your CP PAZ has actually a few important zones in it and it's not really a PAZ, because in a "perfect" CP PAZ price should visit each MPL on the way(pick up the clean orders on the way UP), leaving little or no clean orders behind so that price can travel FREE when it returns to the other end - that's why it's called a PAZ because if price engulfs into it- it travels all the way to the end of it, with or without the necessary retrace that would allow us to join the ride ). The simplest way to start drawing your PAZ's is to find your CP on the chart and then follow the FL trail. Paz's don't align to the PIP because every time an old FL that was the PAZ border gets Engulfed and a new FL is created - this new FL is a bit higher or lower than the original FL it Engulfed. In the recent years some of the IT's have changed a bit the way they mess up with the retailers to fill their position and achieve their plans: they use LS and their Kinks for their entries and for their targets and it's pretty hard to find space between FL's (PAZ) ( most of the engulfs reach their target in the same move leaving us with no PA confirmed opportunity to trade) . In the Very low time frames there are still PA confirmed entries but they require a lot of patience and wisdom. BUT There are times when LS Kinks align with FL's and you can find clean orders inside them close to the MPL(that's your edge of the PAZ) (that's your sweet spot where you want to trade) That's why you'll see that RTM has shifted focus more material HOME EDUCATION FORUM MORE LOGOUT about BSZ and the Traps set by IT and LSK with clean orders inside it in the recent years as it's a more efficient way to extract "safer" pips - As traders our main job is to go where the Opportunities will be...and to adapt our styles to our personality and to the market that we have inherited. Stay thirsty, E.
